## Document 015: 2026-10-14_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］日本の原油輸入価格は今後さらに上昇へ_5c83f50b

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-10-14_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］日本の原油輸入価格は今後さらに上昇へ_5c83f50b.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

みずほ経済 EXPRESS

日本の原油輸入価格は今後さらに上昇へ 
～物価上振れリスクが強まれば、一段の利上げ加速も～

調査部 主任エコノミスト 東深澤 武史 
池田 亮平 
エコノミスト 阿部 大樹 
シニア日本経済エコノミスト 越山 祐資 
080-1069-4919  takeshi.higashifukasawa@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 日銀は物価上振れ抑制に舵。中東情勢に起因する物価押し上げリスクは予断を許さず 
9 月日銀会合後の会見で植田総裁は「政策局面が変化した」と述べた（図表 1） 。これまで日銀は、
基調的物価の押し上げを金融政策運営の重点としてきたが、足元では 2％前後の物価上昇が定着しつ
つあるとみられる。こうしたもとで、金融政策運営の重点は物価上振れの回避に移っており、そのた
めに緩和的な金融環境をなるべく早く脱することが重視される。日銀の基本的なスタンスが、連続利
上げや、0.25％の利上げ幅を上回る大幅利上げは回避するのが望ましいというものであることも踏ま
えると、今後の利上げペースは 3 カ月に一回程度、0.25％ずつが基本になるだろう 1。 
ただし、金融政策の重点が物価上振れ回避にシフトしているなかでは、よりフォワードルッキング
な政策運営が求められる 2。したがって、 原油調達コストの上昇や円安など、 物価上振れリスクの強ま
り次第で利上げペースが速まる可能性がある。先行きの物価を押し上げる主なリスク要因として、日
図表1 9月会合後の総裁会見のポイント 図表2 WTI原油先物

（出所） 日本銀行より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成 （出所） LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 2

2

銀は、原油高等の中東情勢影響、AI 関連需要の強さ、円安の三点を挙げている。このうち、原油価格
は足元で再び上昇しており、先行きの不確実性も大きい。中東情勢は、物価を取り巻く現下最大のリ
スクだろう。 
■ 不安定な情勢下で再び原油価格が上昇。タンカー運賃も急騰しダブルパンチの様相 
WTI原油先物は、9月に入って一時1バレル=100ドル台と、5月中旬以来の水準に上昇した （図表2） 。
足元では原油価格は幾分低下しているものの、水準としては依然高い状態が続いている。背景には、
中東産原油の供給懸念が強まったことがある。7月下旬以降、 サウジアラビアとイエメン・フーシ派に
よる戦闘が開始し、 ホルムズ海峡を代替する重要な原油輸送ルートとして活用されてきたバブ ・ エル ・
マンデブ海峡の通航リスクが高まった。 また、9月に入り、 サウジアラビアの原油パイプラインが攻撃
を受けて停止したと伝わると、中東産原油の域外輸送が一段と困難になるとの見方が広がった。 
中東情勢が再度悪化するなかで上昇しているのは原油価格だけではない。原油価格の上昇を大幅に
上回る勢いで、原油タンカーの運賃が急騰している（図表3）。8月以降に上昇基調が鮮明となり、9月
中旬には一時、中東情勢悪化前（2026年初）の約46倍、情勢悪化後の3～7月頃と比べても約3.2倍に達
した。 海峡航行リスクが高まるもと、 積み替えや迂回によって原油輸送の効率が悪化しているためだ。
海事データを活用して商品市場を分析しているベルギーの調査会社Kplerによると、 ホルムズ海峡の内
側にあるペルシャ湾から原油を運び出す船と、海峡の外側から最終目的地まで運ぶ船を分け、洋上で
原油を積み替える方式が広がっているようだ。 長距離輸送を担う船が危険な航路を通らずに済む反面、
積み替え作業や待機に時間を要するため、船を次の輸送に回しにくくなる。また、危険な海域を避け
るために遠回りする輸送も増え、船が一度の航海に費やす日数も長くなっている。より多くの輸送資
源が必要となったことで船舶需給がひっ迫し、タンカー運賃が大きく押し上げられている格好だ。

図表3 原油タンカーの運賃 図表4 原油輸入単価と原油価格

当たり換算収益。日本を含む東アジア向け運賃の代理指標。

前月比の伸びとして試算。中東情勢悪化を背景に、3月はドバイ原油
の価格形成機能が損なわれたと指摘されているため。原油輸入単価

### Page 3

3

■ 今後は日本の原油調達コストが上昇。企業間取引価格は反転加速を予想 
原油価格の上昇に原油タンカー運賃の急騰が重なり、先行きの日本の原油輸入単価（単位量あたり
の原油調達コスト）は一段と上昇する見込みだ3。 中東産原油が日本に輸送されるまでには1～2カ月程
度の期間を要するため、8月中旬以降の原油価格上昇 ・原油タンカー運賃上昇は10月以降の原油輸入単
価を押し上げ、その後国内の企業間取引価格へと影響が波及していくと考えられる。足元の原油価格
とタンカー運賃を踏まえると、10月の原油輸入単価は1バレル＝約140ドル程度と、中東情勢悪化後で
最も高い水準に上昇する可能性が高い（図表4）。この影響を輸入全体の平均価格（円建てベース）で
みると、10月の伸びは前年比＋29％程度と、中東情勢悪化後に最も輸入物価の伸びが加速した6・7月
に迫る勢いになる計算だ。さらに、その後は、石油化学製品などの関連財を中心に、国内の企業間取
引価格が上昇しよう。 足元で上昇圧力が一服しつつある国内企業物価は再び加速し、11月頃に同＋7％
台の伸びになると予想する（図表5）。 
加えて、原油輸入金額が膨らんで貿易収支が悪化すれば、円安圧力が強まる可能性がある。原油の
代替調達が進展した6月以降は1兆円に迫る貿易赤字が続いているが、特に10月以降は原油輸入単価が
上昇するもとで本邦貿易赤字が拡大する公算が大きく、実需面からの円安圧力が強まりやすい（図表
6） 。 足元の為替相場は、 米国の利上げ観測を背景に円安に振れやすい地合いだ。 そこに実需の動きが
重なり、1ドル＝160円を上回るようなドル高円安が進行すれば、輸入物価の上振れを通じて、国内企
業物価の伸びが一層高まるリスクがある。 
■ 物価上昇圧力が長期化する場合は利上げペース加速も視野に 
以上のように、当面の企業間取引価格に対しては押し上げ圧力が持続する公算が大きい。物価上昇
圧力が長期化するほど消費者物価への価格転嫁姿勢も強まる可能性があるだけに、日銀は物価上振れ
に対する高い警戒レベルを維持することになろう。 
仮に物価上振れリスクが大きく高まる場合には、 連続利上げ ・大幅利上げの可能性も排除できない。
中東情勢の混乱が長期化し、27年以降も原油輸入単価が一段と上振れることが見込まれる場合、ある
図表5 輸入物価（円建て）と国内企業物価 図表6 貿易収支

### Page 4

4

いは1ドル=160円を持続的に上回る水準まで円安が進展する場合には 、連続利上げや大幅利上げのシ
ナリオが現実味を帯びてくるだろう。

1 中立金利の水準は不透明であり、利上げの影響はタイムラグをともなって現れる。連続利上げ・大幅利上げによって意図せず
金融引き締めに転じれば、経済へのマイナス影響が必要以上に強まるリスクがある。 
2 中東情勢悪化以降で最初の利上げを決定した 6 月会合を振り返っても、日銀は川上の企業物価の加速を理由の一つとして利上
げに踏み切っている。6 月会合の声明文において日銀は、国内企業物価上昇率が前年比＋6.3％（5 月時点の速報ベース）に加速
したことを踏まえ、 「企業間取引におけるやや速いスピードでの価格転嫁が、消費者段階における幅広い品目の価格上昇に波及
していく可能性がある」と評価した。 
3 中東情勢悪化以降、日本の原油輸入単価は国際市況から大きく上振れている。背景として、主な代替調達先である米国産原油
が需給ひっ迫の懸念等から値上がりし、割高になったことなどが挙げられる。経済産業省発表によると、秋口以降は中東産原油
の調達が増加し、割高な米国産原油に対する依存度は低下すると見込まれるものの、本文記載のように中東産原油の輸送コスト
が大幅に高まっていることから、先行きも割高感の解消は見込みづらい。

市場焦點
美國
• 9月CPI年增3.7%、PPI年增6.3%，雙雙高於前期 ，能源價格的急升是通膨回升的主要推力 。
雖然核心CPI年增率僅升至2.6%，但住房與服務價格仍維持黏著，使通膨降溫過程出現反覆；
同時PPI增幅明顯高於 CPI，反映企業成本壓力偏高 ，未來不排除進一步向終端價格傳導 。整
體而言，CPI與PPI同步回升反映通膨降溫進程仍存在不確定性 ，進而令市場重估通膨回落速
度與對Fed貨幣政策路徑的審慎預期。
AI
• AI雲端業者 CoreWeave的5年期CDS利差一度突破 1,140個基點 ，旗下兩檔債券殖利率逼近
15%，相關賣壓並進一步蔓延至股票市場 。全球最大半導體設備商ASML表示，客戶已開始洽
談2029至2030年的訂單；晶圓代工龍頭台積電財報亦優於市場預期 。與此同時，台灣出口增

速創3月以來新高，韓國9月半導體出口年增逾260%，顯示半導體需求持續強勁，預期第三季
相關企業獲利仍有望維持強勁。
中國
• 9月通膨加速主要源於中東能源衝擊及 AI硬體需求，能源和科技領域之外 ，普遍性的通縮壓力
仍在，彭博表示以GDP平減指數衡量 ，經歷三年下滑後 ，整體經濟預計將在 2026年走出通縮 ，
投資上短期可聚焦，受惠AI資本支出與能源價格上行的半導體、伺服器及能源相關族群。

美股獲利引擎轉向半導體/核心AI與能源
• 雖然標普500指數在2026年第三季展現出獲利韌性 ，但隨著油價上漲侵蝕利潤率 、消費者受通膨壓抑 ，以及分析師下調未
來預測動能，市場未來的容錯空間正在縮小。
• AI與半導體仍為獲利領頭羊：科技與半導體持續為標普500指數獲利成長的核心動力，其中約20家企業組成的核心AI一籃子
股票(權重約34.6%)對指數獲利的貢獻度已超過科技七雄(Mag 7)，且此優勢預計將至少延續至2028年底。
• 油價推升能源產業，接棒成為強棒：受到近期國際油價上揚影響，能源產業獲利預測顯著上修，預計獲利成長勁道將延續至
2027年初。在接下來的三個季度中，能源板塊的收益成長表現預計將超越科技七雄。
• 消費與運輸業面臨成本壓頂：相較於科技與能源的強勁，必需消費品與交通運輸業正承受顯著壓力。例如，達美航空受高燃

料成本拖累，而Nike與百事可樂則反映出消費者因持續通膨而縮減支出的困境。

利潤率高檔盤整、關稅退款帶來短補，但動能已見放緩
• 利潤率高檔盤整，淨利潤率提升受限：標普500成分股第二季營運利潤率(18.7%)與淨利潤率(15.6%)均創下新高。雖然第三
季營運利潤率預估可進一步提升至19.4%，但淨利潤率預計微幅上升至15.8%，顯示整體企業淨利潤的擴張動能正漸趨平緩 。
• 700億美元關稅退款提供短線補藥： 受惠於美國政府依據國際緊急經濟權力法 (IEEPA)的追償退稅，估計仍有至少700億美元
的關稅款將退還給標普 500成分股(主要集中於工業、非必需消費及醫療保健等 )，這預計能為2026年標普500指數每股收益
(EPS)成長帶來約235個基點、利潤率提升約34個基點的短期提振。
• 上修動能開始轉弱：雖然整體財報指引動量仍維持正值，但過去六週內，分析師對未來12個月EPS與收入的修正動能出現頻

繁下修，這意味著市場先前的極度樂觀情緒正在消退，未來財報季度要優於預期的門檻正在提高。
• 小結與投資建議： 標普500指數短期內靠著半導體 /AI與能源雙引擎拉抬 ，加上關稅退款的一次性利多注入 ，下半年整體獲
利依然具備支撐。然而，高油價帶來的營運成本上升 ，以及終端消費力道的疲軟 ，正逐漸侵蝕非科技/非能源企業的利潤 ，
而大盤廣度不足(僅少數板塊支撐獲利)，代表未來投資風險正逐漸集中 ，但AI算力需求仍屬硬性需求，但波動加劇下，投資
建議以定期定額方式投資美股核心AI與半導體龍頭股票。

美國9月CPI、PPI 同步升溫，能源衝擊擴大通膨與利率風險
• 能源價格急升，消費通膨再升溫：美國9月CPI月增0.6%、
年增3.7%，高於前月 3.4%，其中汽油月增 9.1%、能源
價格月增 5.2%，汽油單項即貢獻整體 CPI當月漲幅逾半；
另一方面，核心CPI月增0.3%、年增2.6%、住房指數月
增0.4%，顯示能源雖是本次通膨回升主因 ，但住房及部
分服務價格仍具黏著性，令通膨降溫進程面臨反覆。
• 上游價格大幅攀升 ，成本傳導風險升高： 9月PPI最終需
求月增1.3%、年增6.3%，創2022年3月以來最大增幅，
其中最終需求商品月增 2.6%，能源價格大漲 10.8%，汽
油價格更跳升 20.3%；核心PPI月增0.5%、年增5.0%，
顯示價格壓力不僅來自能源 ，也涉及部分商品與服務環

節；後續是否進一步推升至 CPI，仍須觀察企業成本轉
嫁程度及需求變化。
• 市場看法： 通膨回升恐提高 Fed再度升息機率 ，並延長
高利率維持時間 ，甚至不排除進一步緊縮的可能 ，短期
有利支撐美債殖利率及美元表現 ，不過股、債市後續走
勢仍將受到貨幣政策預期與風險情緒變化影響 。資產配
置建議可關注具成本轉嫁能力 、現金流穩健的能源類股；
債券則宜以短中天期 、高信用評等標的為主 ，以降低利
率敏感度。黃金可作為分散地緣政治及通膨風險的輔助
配置，但不宜將任何單一資產視為必然受惠標的。

供給面推動中國9月CPI升0.9%
• 供給面推動中國9月通膨復甦：中國9月通膨數據顯示，CPI年增率由8月的0.8%升至0.9%，連續兩個月擴大，但低於市場預期
的1.0%，物價上漲主要受到能源及旅遊價格帶動，而食品價格跌幅進一步擴大，使整體通膨壓力仍相對溫和，扣除食品與能源
後，核心CPI維持在1.0%，反映內需需求尚未明顯升溫 。另一方面，PPI年增率由3.8%大幅升至5.0%，優於市場預期，且月增
率達1.2%，創4月以來最大漲幅，顯示上游原物料及工業品價格回升，須關注未來是否逐步傳導至消費端價格。
• 展望後市： 9月通膨加速主要源於中東能源衝擊及 AI硬體需求 ，能源和科技領域之外 ，普遍性的通縮壓力仍 在，彭博表示 以
GDP平減指數衡量，經歷三年下滑後，整體經濟預計將在2026年走出通縮，值得注意的是，這種供給面驅動的通膨復甦，將降

低官方推出需求刺激措施的緊迫感 ，而要反轉通縮循環正需要官方舉措，投資上短期可聚焦受惠AI資本支出與能源價格上行的
半導體、伺服器及能源相關族群。
資料來源：彭博，2026.10.14

過去18個月來，新興市場股票表現亮眼，累積報酬率超過標普500指數
的兩倍
• 過去18個月來，新興市場股票表現亮眼，累積報酬率超過標普500指數的兩倍：主因為獲利帶動成長，整體而言，新興市場企
業獲利表現強勁 ，而且仍持續加速成長 ，高盛預估2026年新興市場企業盈餘成長率將達 55%，超過標普500企業盈餘成長速
度的兩倍，且新興市場股票估值較美國股市存在超過40%的折價空間，評價相對具吸引力，今年以來流入新興市場股市的資金
已接近500億美元，創下過去20年來最強勁紀錄之一。更重要的是新興市場國家目前貢獻了全球近 80%的AI硬體出口。隨著AI
產業持續發展，受惠範圍有望從少數龍頭企業擴大至更多國家及公司，特別是那些能夠協助解決供應鏈瓶頸的企業。
• 結論：後市來看在AI產業趨勢、相對低估的評價水準，以及強勁企業獲利成長三大因素支撐下，新興市場股票未來一年仍具備

進一步上漲的潛力，有機會延續目前的強勢表現。

AI投資支撐美國成長，融資需求升高使長端利率與信用風險並存
• AI基礎建設持續擴張 ，成為企業投資重要動能 ：大型雲端業者
持續投入資料中心 、半導體、網路設備與電力基礎設施 ，帶動
相關產業資本支出與供應鏈需求 。摩根大通資產管理指出 ，納
入資料中心開發商及半導體投資後，AI 基礎建設至 2030 年的
累計投資需求可能達約 5兆美元 ，顯示AI投資循環仍具規模 ，
但實際經濟貢獻與企業獲利 ，仍取決於投資效率及商業化成果 。
• 外部融資需求增加 ，債券供給與投資回報成關鍵： 隨AI投資規
模擴大，大型科技企業逐步增加公司債與外部融資 ，在企業債
與公債供給同步增加下 ，長天期利率面臨上行壓力 。若未來AI
獲利成長未能跟上資本支出擴張速度 ，可能壓縮自由現金流與

資本報酬率，進一步擴大信用利差 ，並提高高估值科技股的評
價修正風險
• 市場觀察：AI基礎建設仍是未來數年推動全球科技投資與企業
數位轉型的成長主軸，相關需求具備中長期發展空間，而2026
年市場已逐漸轉向檢視投資回報率 、現金流創造能力及融資結
構是否具備可持續性 ，因此單純依賴資本支出擴張已不足以支
撐所有AI概念股評價 。建議配置可兼顧具獲利與現金流支撐的
AI供應鏈，以及高信用評等 、中短天期債券；對長天期債券則
須評估供給與利率風險 ，並審慎檢視高負債 AI企業的償債能力
及投資回報。
資料來源：Goldman Sachs，永豐金證券， 2026.10.12

AI熱潮、高債務與網路威脅，IMF示警三大金融穩定風險升高
資料來源：Bloomberg Finance L.P， Dealogic，Zero day Clock and IMF staff calculations.， 2026.10.13
AI相關風險持續升溫 主權債券殖利率攀升 網路資安威脅升高
大型雲端科技企業債券發行規模
（占投資等級公司債市場比重) 10年期公債殖利率平均變動幅度 零時差漏洞占比（%）
• AI估值高度集中，市場重定價風險升溫：IMF於10月《全球金融穩定報告》指出，AI相關投資已成為全球成長的重要支撐，
但資金與漲勢高度集中於少數企業及市場，一旦獲利成長不如預期或估值遭重新檢視，可能引發科技股修正並透過風險偏好
下降擴散至整體金融市場。
• 通膨與財政疑慮恐推升債券市場波動： IMF認為，若能源價格推升通膨壓力或市場對財政永續性產生疑慮 ，主權債殖利率可

能進一步上升，進而提高全球融資成本與資產重估風險；在高利率與高債務環境下，金融條件快速緊縮的情況將明顯提高。
• 網路安全風險成為潛在威脅： 隨金融體系數位化程度提高 ，IMF將大型網路攻擊列為重要風險來源之一 。若攻擊事件波及支
付系統、金融基礎設施或雲端服務平台，不僅可能影響市場運作效率，也可能透過信心衝擊與流動性壓力擴大金融市場波動。
• 投資建議：市場成長動能仍受AI支撐，但在金融穩定風險上升情境下，建議採取「AI受惠股＋高品質債券＋防禦型資產」的
槓鈴策略；核心配置可聚焦全球AI基礎建設供應鏈及投資等級債，藉由企業長期獲利成長與債券避險功能，同時因應估值修
正及金融條件收緊風險。

AI隱憂浮現但動能仍在
• CoreWeave信用風險亮紅燈：市場憂心雲端業者在算力租賃業務產生足夠回報前，恐難以負擔龐大債務，導致信用風險升
溫。上週，AI雲端業者CoreWeave的5年期信用違約交換（CDS）利差一度突破1,140個基點，升至通常反映嚴重信用壓力
的水準；旗下兩檔債券殖利率逼近15%，其中2032年到期的無擔保債券於上週三跌破面值的80%，相關賣壓並進一步蔓延
至股票市場。
• AI半導體產業的基本面仍具韌性：全球最大半導體設備商艾司摩爾（ASML）表示，客戶已開始洽談2029至2030年的訂單；
晶圓代工龍頭台積電財報亦優於市場預期。與此同時，台灣出口增速創3月以來新高，韓國9月半導體出口年增逾260%，顯
示半導體需求持續強勁，預期第三季相關企業獲利仍有望維持強勁。

美銀美林調查指出，全球經理人對景氣與股市仍維持偏多立場
資料來源：BofA Global Fund Manager Survey，2026.10
• 經理人樂觀情緒維持高檔： 美銀美林10月全球基金經理人調查顯示 ，市場風險偏好仍高 ，平均現金水位維持在 3.9%的低檔，
科技股配置則升至2020年8月以來最高，反映經理人對AI產業及企業獲利前景仍具信心 。然而，整體經濟成長預期已轉趨保
守，淨4%的受訪者預期未來 12個月經濟成長放緩 。極端風險方面，債券殖利率無序攀升仍居首位 ，占比31%；AI泡沫及通
膨再起分別為23%及20%。此外，39%的經理人認為，大型雲端業者資本支出是系統性信用事件的主要潛在來源 ，顯示市場
焦點已從AI需求成長延伸至龐大資本支出的獲利回收能力。

• 美國公債殖利率走高 ，債券的收益吸引力逐漸回升： 調查顯示，37%的受訪者表示，當美國10年期公債殖利率升至 6%時，
才會考慮由股票轉向債券；另有19%認為目前殖利率已具吸引力，顯示部分經理人已開始重新評估固定收益資產的配置價值 。
不過，債券整體仍處淨減碼，且經理人對科技股的配置持續升高 ，反映市場尚未全面轉向防禦。後續仍須關注美債殖利率變
化、AI資本支出的投資回報，以及企業獲利能否支撐偏高的市場評價。
資料來源：BofA Global Fund Manager Survey，2026.10

台積電財報佳卻未激勵股價，短線面臨總經干擾
• 台積電Q3營收、獲利及EPS同步改寫單季新高：營收年增50.9%、季增17.6%，稅後純益7,630.6億元，年增68.7％、季增8
％；每股稅後純益(EPS)29.43元，整體優於市場預期，但毛利率66.4％較第二季的67.7％下降。
• 財測展望：第四季財測營收預估 523~543億美元，季增上看15％、年增65％，毛利率預估65%~67%，反映高階晶片需求
依然強勁。全年展望方面，預期2026年美元營收年增約44%，較上季預估的「略高於40%」說法更加明確。此外，重申全
年資本支出600~640億美元不變。
• 市場反應：台積電法說會隔日股價小幅下跌，法說會未出現激勵行情，主要原因包含美國通膨升溫壓力、外資連續調節部位，

以及毛利率略為下降。短線籌碼面可能仍會調整，但台積電董事長魏哲家在法說會中強調，第二波AI需求正強勁興起，台積
電正透過多種途徑全力擴充產能，顯示高階晶片前景明確，中長期具投資價值，多家投資機構也紛紛上調目標價。
資料來源：台積電法說會，兆豐銀行整理，2026.10.15。
法說會重點整理 說明
AI需求持續升溫
魏哲家坦言，目前仍難以判斷晶圓供需缺口將擴大或縮小，因為
客戶需求持續增加，台積電也正積極提高產能。儘管消費性電子
市場仍受到零組件漲價及總體經濟不確定性影響，但先進製程需
求依然強勁，公司預估2026年全年美元營收將年增約44％。
擴廠進度
(1)台南科學園區新建3奈米廠預計2027年下半年量產，並決定在
台南再增建一座3奈米廠，規畫2028年投入量產。
(2)美國亞利桑那州第二座晶圓廠已完成廠房興建，預計2027年
第三季量產，時程較原先規畫略為提前；日本第二座晶圓廠則將
導入3奈米製程，預計2028年量產。
台積電證實洽談Terafab
合作
魏哲家強調，目前相關合作仍在討論階段，尚未有結論。不過，

無論未來採取何種合作模式，晶圓廠都必須由台積電負責營運。
電力供應疑慮
美國電力供應情況是公司評估AI長期需求的重要因素之一，因此
台積電進一步確認當地電力供應狀況，目前相關研究及評估結果
均良好，尚未發現明顯問題。

• 全球經濟正處於「韌性仍在、風險升高」的階段：國際貨幣基金組織(IMF)指出，AI投資熱潮持續推動美國、台灣等國家成
長，但中東地緣政治衝突、高通膨風險、政府債務問題與AI泡沫化疑慮，也同時為市場埋下不確定因素。
• 市場永遠充滿雜音，定期定額紀律投資更具優勢：本許多人認為定期定額的核心在於「平均成本」，但更重要的是幫助投資
人克服人性。市場上漲時持續投入，可以參與企業獲利成長帶來的成果，市場下跌時持續扣款，則能以較低價格累積更多單
位數。換句話說，定期定額讓投資人不需要每天判斷：明天會不會跌？現在是不是太貴？要不要先停扣觀望？長期而言，紀

律投資往往比短期擇時的成效更佳。
• AI時代更適合定期定額：儘管IMF認為人工智慧發展為全球經濟成長帶來貢獻，但同時也警告，若市場對AI未來獲利能力過
度樂觀，不排除出現股市估值修正。根據歷史經驗，每一次市場重大修正往往也孕育下一輪成長契機，不論AI發展是否出現
泡沫，善用定期定額策略可以在機會與風險之間達到平衡。
韌性仍在、風險升溫，紀律投資更穩健
任一月份定期定額進場投資試算
註：以MSCI全球指數2010/1/31~2025/12/31試算。假設定期定額每月月底投入、
原幣計算。指數資料試算僅供參考，非投資商品之實際績效。資料來源Bloomberg。

非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良

息日前一日之淨值×100%。

⚫ 歐元區 9 月 ZEW 經濟景氣指數自前月的 31.4 降至 25.8，低於預估的 39.9。數
據下滑顯示在美伊衝突未獲緩解 、 能源價格持續高位情況下 ， 市場對於歐元區經
濟前景抱持較不樂觀的態度。
⚫ 美國公布初請失業金人數為 19.6 萬人，優於市場預期的 20.8 萬，再度呼應聯準
會主席華許(Kevin Warsh)認為目前勞動力市場仍具韌性的說法， 在美國經濟穩
健及通膨頑固的情況下，預計聯準會將持續升息，有利美元持穩。
⚫ 歐洲央行管委暨葡萄牙央行總裁佩雷拉(Alvaro Santos Pereira)表示，歐元區目前

通膨雖未廣泛擴散 ， 惟考量近期天然氣價格飆升 ， 央行將密切關注未來通膨壓力
擴散情形。最新利率期貨市場顯示，歐洲央行今年底仍有再升息一碼空間。
⚫ 日本政府通過消費稅減稅及家庭現金補貼的政策大綱，預計自明年 4 月起的兩
年內，將食品消費稅率自 8%降至 1%，惟大綱中未明確說明資金來源，再度引
發市場對於日本政府的財政擔憂，短線不利日圓表現。
⚫ 台灣央行 9/17 宣布重貼現率連 10 凍維持在 2%不變，央行總裁楊金龍表示，台
灣的通膨相較其他主要經濟體溫和，採取漸進緊縮貨幣政策有助物價穩定。 此
外，目前通膨率為 2%，明年有望降至 2%以下，後續將觀察通膨發展、主要經
濟體貨幣政策緊縮程度及台灣金融情勢，以決定未來貨幣政策走向。

 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.650，低點在 31.958，以 31.808 作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 1 角 7 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶 ， 美國CPI 數據公布後加強市場對於聯準會 9 月升息的預期 ，
早盤台幣跳空開低，外資連續三日賣超台股並匯出，加以出口商態度轉為惜售，令
台幣接連貶破 31.700、31.800、31.900 關卡， 週四由於聯準會偏鷹派升息，美元指
數走升至 100.300，早盤台幣跳空開低，台幣最低貶至31.958，不過隨後出口商賣壓
出籠，外資雙向進出，台幣收復部分跌幅，維持在31.900 附近震盪；臨近周末，外
資買超台股並於外匯市場匯入 ， 台幣走升至31.722， 最終在央行調節之下收在31.808。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪 。 就國際市場而言 ， 美國最新初領失業救濟金人數較預
期減少一萬多人、續領失業救濟金人數亦下降，顯示美國勞動力市場尚屬穩健，加
以聯準會升息抗通膨，鷹派立場給予美元指數支撐、不利台幣表現；就台灣市場來
看，九月台灣央行並未升息，台美利差擴大預計將帶給台幣壓力，此外，儘管聯準
會升息利空出盡，外資買超台股帶動台幣走升，惟台灣即將迎來中秋連假，預計金
融市場交投謹慎，將令台幣陷入橫盤整理格局，綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢區間
震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

卡，顯示 USD/CNH 空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣震盪走升。

美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/21/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週日圓走貶。 週初，美國通膨數據維持高位 ，令聯準會升息預期升溫 ，USD/JPY
自 153.29 低點反彈至 155 附近；週間 ，美國聯準會宣布升息一碼 ， 帶動USD/JPY 上
漲至 156.41；臨近週末，日本央行如市場預期升息一碼，惟央行偏鴿派立場令日圓
續貶，帶動 USD/JPY 一度最高來到 158.05，而隨後在日本政府於外匯市場進行匯率
詢價下，USD/JPY 回吐部分漲幅，終場收在 156.86，週線上漲 349 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本政府上周通過消費稅減
稅及家庭現金補貼政策大綱，惟大綱中未明確說明財源，再度引發市場對於日本政
府財政惡化的擔憂，預計將不利日圓走勢。二、日本央行上周五決議將政策利率調
升一碼至 1.25%， 9 名決策委員中有 2 人對升息持反對意見，降低市場對日本央行
進一步升息的預期。另一方面 ， 聯準會上周三亦升息一碼至3.75%-4.00%區間 ，FOMC
政策聲明顯示，18 位官員中有 12 人預計今年將再升息一次，另有 4 人認為存在升
息兩次的可能性，聯準會升息立場明顯較日本央行更為鷹派，預計將支撐USD/JPY
走勢。三、日本財務大臣片山皋月近期再度重申，將於必要時採取外匯干預行動，
以維護外匯市場有序運行。四、就技術面而言，在日線圖中，USD/JPY 短中天期均
線向下發散走勢偏空，惟近期匯價在多次向下測試 154 均拉回，明顯下方有買盤支
撐。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

歐洲央行今年仍有升息一碼的空間，預計將支撐歐元走勢。二、美國聯準會決議將
利率調升一碼至 3.75%-4.00%區間，聯準會主席華許會後偏鷹言論帶動美元指數走
升至 100 上方，預計將限縮歐元走升空間。三、歐元區 9 月 ZEW 經濟景氣指數自
前月的 31.4 降至 25.8，低於預估的 39.9。數據下滑顯示在美伊衝突未獲緩解、能源
價格持續高位情況下，市場對於歐元區經濟前景抱持較不樂觀的態度，將不利歐元
表現。四、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 近期回測年線未能有效突破後明顯
拉回轉為下行走勢，目前匯價承壓於各天期均線下方，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

盤整格局，後續關注下週三財政部公布的第四季公債發行計劃，預估 5 年券
區間為 1.79%~1.83%，10 年券區間為 1.90%~1.95%。

4Q26/2027 年獲利將續創新高，受惠 DDR4 供不應求延續 
預估 4Q26 營收季增 13.2%至 1,512.46 億 元 ， 其 中 預 期ASP 再 季 增
15~20%、雖收斂但仍維持雙位數季增；毛利率季增 0.4ppts 至 86.1%，報
價維持高檔下與 3Q26 相近；EPS 季增 11.9%至 28.42 元。展望後續，月產
能未來四年內將由目前約 65kwpm 分階段新增 35.9kwpm 的產能、2H27 開
始貢獻，供需緊俏延續下 ASP 將維持高檔，4Q26/2027 年營運續創新高。 
2027 年 DRAM 供需緊俏與報價上行持續，主要由 Server 驅動 
本中心預期 2027 年 DRAM 持續供不應求，報價仍在上行循環，最主要驅動
力 Server 於整體位元需求佔比過半且供需差達 20%以上(高於整體 DRAM
的約 10%)，其中預期 2027 年 HBM 市場規模年增 243%，Nvidia VR200 搭
載 SOCAMM2 位元需求成長達 6 倍，General Server 亦佔整體 Server 需求
一半比重，相關 RDIMM、LPDRAM 成長強勁；同時新增產能相對需求成長
仍有限，三大原廠 2027 年底 DRAM 投片月產能僅年增 10~15%，中國同業
長鑫存儲雖擴產積極仍僅佔整體產能約 15%，主要供應中國國內需求。此外，
DDR4 於消費性/網通/Server 仍具龐大需求，目前 AI Server 仍有約 10%週
邊應用搭載 DDR4，供給則在國際原廠 EOL 下大幅縮減，長鑫存儲重心亦
已轉往 DDR5，預估 2027 年 DDR4 仍有 20%以上的供需缺口(詳見圖 7)。 
產品組合持續往 Server/AI 提升，中長期營運展望正向 
綜 合 上 述 ， 預 估2026/27 年 營 收 年 增 525.5%/66.8% 至 4,165.31 億元
/6,946.17 億元 ，毛利率年增 60ppts/2ppts 至 82.5%/84.5% ，EPS 年增
3457.6%/68.6%至 75.93/128.01 元。考量 1) 公司現為 DDR4 第一大廠且同
時具備 DDR5 產品布局、2) 產品組合持續往 Server/AI 提升、3) WoW 客製
化產品有望帶來更穩定獲利支撐，中長期營運更加穩健，以 2.5 倍 PB(過去
三年落在 0.4~2.8 倍)、2027 年 BVPS 2 30.7 元，給予買進、目標價 570 元。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
4Q26 預期 ASP 再季增 15~20%，2027 年 DDR4 供不應求延續下合約
價仍維持高檔，獲利將年增 68.6%續創新高。 
⧫  
2027 年 DRAM 產業 供 需 緊 俏 及 報 價 上 行 持 續 ， 最 主 要 驅 動 力 為
Server 相關 DRAM 訂單需求大幅成長，新增產能則相對有限。 
⧫  
產品組合續往 Server/AI 提升，中長期營運更加穩健，給予買進、目標
價 570 元(2.5x 2027 年 BVPS)。

營運分析
3Q26 獲利季增 74.6%，主因 ASP 季增>60%
3Q26 營收 1,336.49 億元，季增 61.9%/年增 611.7%，其中 1) 平均售價(ASP)：季增超過 60%/年
增超過 500%， 受 惠 半 年 期/一年期等 LTA 重 新 議 價 及 季 度 合 約 價 調 整 、2) 位 元 出 貨 量(Bit
shipment)：季減中個位數百分比 /年減低個位數百分比，主因新廠產能尚未開出且庫存處於低水位；
毛利率達 85.7%，季增 6.2ppts/年增 67.2ppts，受惠 ASP 大幅成長；營業利益率 80%，季增
6.3ppts/年增 74ppts，費用方面提列當期及未來之員工薪酬增加；稅後獲利 876.27 億元，季增
74.6%/年增 5505.1%；EPS 25.40 元，整體 3Q26 獲利大致符合市場預期。

供不應求延續下，2027 年 DRAM 合約價仍維持高檔且獲利續創新高
本次法說會重點包括：1) 公司表示 DDR4 市場環境健康，DDR4 目前佔整體 DRAM 市場需求仍有
約 12~14%，雖需求逐漸縮小但對應大幅縮減的供給仍相當強勁，ASP 仍有望保持雙位數成長；2)
2026 年資本支出指引不超過新台幣 697 億元，2026~2029 年 5A 新廠資本支出 預算約新台幣
3,466 億元，屏東新廠長期資本支出規劃達 100 億美元，主要用於 AI 應用的 WoW 3D 晶圓封裝與

測試需求，預計 2H28 起設備裝機； 3) Server/AI 相關目前營收占比 >20%，公司指引將成長至
2029~2030 年的 40~50%，預期隨新產能上線持續提升。
展望 2026/2027 年，目前月產能推估約 65kwpm，而 5A 新廠具備約 50kwpm 的擴充空間，目前規
劃 2028 年將投產至 30kwpm，新增產能預計於 2H27 開始微幅貢獻，相關資本支出預算約新台幣
3,466 億元(含 EUV 設備)，長期而言公司亦預計於屏東科學園區投資設立 3D 晶圓封裝測試廠，導
入矽穿孔(TSV)、晶圓堆疊等 3DIC 先進技術；位元出貨量方面，2026 年在晶圓產出尚待新廠產能
開出、以生產效率提升與 16nm 製程微幅增加貢獻下，公司維持年增 high-10s % 的指引，2027 年
考量高基期且新廠產能於下半年才開始貢獻，預估 2H27 前位元出貨量維持平穩。
ASP 則受惠市場供需緊俏，1~3Q26 每季皆季增約 60~70%，4Q26 估亦將維持 15~20%的雙位數

季增幅；進入 2027 年，DDR4 供不應求態勢延續，但考量報價已處高檔、非 AI 下游終端漸難以負
荷，且記憶體原廠毛利率多已達 80%以上，預估 2027 年各季 ASP 持平至季增個位數百分比。
綜合上述，預估 2026/2027 年營收年增 525.5%/66.8%至 4,165.31 億元/6,946.17 億元；毛利率年
增 60ppts/2ppts 至 82.5%/84.5%，2026 年毛利率受惠 ASP 大幅年增已顯著提升至高水位，2027
年報價上行趨勢持續但逐漸持穩於高檔，折舊方面， 2026 年公司預估資本支出以不超過 新台幣
697 億元為上限，其中約 50%用於設備採購，2027 年隨 5A 新廠投產所需設備投資持續增加，資
本支出將大幅年增，故在 2026 年隨先前廠房設備折舊陸續到期使折舊成本相對低之後，2027 年折
舊金額將明顯增加，但在目前 ASP 與獲利皆維持高檔且加計新產出貢獻下，整體 2027 年毛利率受
折舊影響可控；營益率年增 68.8ppts/2.2ppts 至 76.7%/78.9%；稅後獲利年增 3861%/68.6%至

2,619.4 億元/4,416.29 億元；EPS 75.93/128.01 元。
除報價與產能拉升外，中長期可關注趨勢包括： 1) Server/AI 相關應用占比持續提升、 2) DDR5
16Gb 6400/7200Mbps 及以上貢獻增加、3) WoW 客製化高頻寬記憶體放量。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第5 頁，共 20頁  
產品結構分析 
受惠 DDR4 供需緊俏及寡佔優勢、Server/AI 占比續增，亦具備 DDR5 產品布局 
雖三大國際原廠 DDR4 EOL 以將產能轉向 HBM/DDR5，中國同業長鑫存儲(CXMT)亦將重心轉往
DDR5，但 DDR4 於消費性/網通/Server 等仍具龐大需求，在供給大幅縮減下，公司目前已為全球
第一大 DDR4 供應商，高度寡占且具備價格主導權，除 8Gb DDR4 以外，16Gb 亦於 2H26 開始貢
獻營收。公司目前 Server/AI 相關應用營收比重已超過 20%，且占比將隨新產能開出而提升，持續
受惠 Storage、BMC、NIC、Switch 應用等貢獻，相較國內同業有望減緩消費性疲弱影響。 
DDR4 為公司目前產業地位、獲利最有利的產品組合，但其亦具備 DDR5 技術能力與相關營收貢獻，
目前採 1B 製程的 16Gb DDR5 已量產出貨，傳輸速率現以 6400Mbps 為主並持續開發 7200Mbps
及以上規格。未來若終端客戶需求加速由 DDR4 轉向 DDR5，公司得以靈活調整產能配置以增加出
貨，持續拿下市場份額。除一般型 DRAM 以外，公司亦有 LPDDR4 產品出貨，LPDDR5 則有望於
2027 年導入量產。 
圖 8：目前營收仍以 DDR4 為主

圖 9：1B/1C 營收占比持續提升

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧整理 資料來源：元大投顧預估 
WoW 客製化高頻寬記憶體為長期成長利基，布局 Edge AI 與外溢市場需求 
WoW(Wafer-on-Wafer)客製化高頻寬記憶體為有利公司中長期營運體質的重要成長動能。目前的標
準 HBM 為 IC 對 IC 堆疊，主要用於 Cloud AI 訓練等市場，然而 Edge 端裝置受限於功耗、成本與
物理體積等限制無法直接採用，同時三大國際原廠有限之 HBM 產能亦無法完全滿足整體 Cloud 端
市場需求，公司亦將受惠此外溢效果。其客製化產品採用 Wafer-on-Wafer Hybrid Bonding( 晶圓級
混合鍵合)技術，將 DRAM 與邏輯 Core Die 直接對接堆疊，能以顯著之成本與功耗優勢滿足客戶於
Edge 端的高頻寬需求。目前有多個客戶專案進行中，預估 2H27~2028 年可見較清晰的量產營收，
新廠產能規劃上亦保留彈性予 WoW 客製化 DRAM 專案，長線有望為公司提供較穩定的獲利支撐。 
圖 10：Wafer-on-wafer 堆疊技術示意圖

資料來源：Samsung、元大投顧整理

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第6 頁，共 20頁  
財務分析 
整體財務結構於產業上行及下行循環期間皆維持穩健 
公司在 2020~2022 年 上 行 循 環 期 間 ， 流 動 比 率 與 速 動 比 率 分 別 高 達2020/2021/2022 年的
874%/678%/652%及 707%/599%/502%，後續下行期間雖降至 2023/2024 年的 480%/329%及
339%/217%，但整體仍位於健康水準，且負債比率在上行及下行期間均維持在低於 20%的低水準，
短長期償債能力無虞。2025 年以來隨著記憶體市況轉佳、報價顯著上漲，加上 1H26 完成 787.18
億元之私募案，流動比率與速動比率再回升至 2Q26 的 691%與 635%，負債比率則為 17%，預期
後續持續優化。 
經營能力方面，應收/應付帳款週轉天數落在 36~60 天/19~48 天的健康區間，且兩者差距在合理範
圍內。然而，存貨週轉天數受記憶體景氣循環影響，由上行循環期間 2020/2021 年的 130/97 天，
攀升至下行循環期間 2023/2024 年的 271/333 天，使整體現金轉換週期由 2021 年的 120 天拉長至
2024 年 337 天，反映景氣谷底期間庫存積壓對營運資金運轉效率帶來短期壓力。不過隨著 2025
年以來 DRAM 報價顯著上漲且公司庫存去化加速，2Q26 存貨週轉天數已降至 124 天，帶動現金轉
換週期縮短至 118 天，預期將持續改善。 
觀察現金流與資本支出動向，公司於 2021 年達到 328.47 億元的自由現金流高峰，隨後於
2022~2024 年下行期間，公司雖降低但仍投入 100 多億元的資本支出以推動製程技術組合轉往
10nm 級發展，自由現金流 2023/2024 年轉為淨流出 193.4/141.91 億元。儘管自由現金流短暫承壓，
公司在手現金仍穩健維持於 500 億元以上之充沛水準， 2025 年進入上行循環後營運顯著轉佳，
1H26 自由現金流已達 805.52 億元，加上引進 Kioxia、SanDisk、Solidigm 及 Cisco 之高達 787.18
億元的私募案挹注，使 2Q26 底在手現金大幅提升至 2,176.03 億元的歷史新高，且隨著營運成長
將持續增加，足以支應 5A 新廠未來四年高達 3,466 億元之資本支出規劃，整體財務結構穩健。

產業概況  
2027 年 DRAM 供需緊俏與報價上行持續，主要由 Server 需求驅動 
本中心預期 2027 年 DRAM 將持續供不應求，報價仍在上行循環，其中最主要驅動力為 Server 相
關 DRAM 訂單需求大幅成長且優於預期，Server 佔整體 DRAM 位元需求比重持續增加，預期自
2026 年的 54%成長至 2027 年的 63%，供需差更達 20%以上，高於整體 DRAM 的約 10%，主因：
1) Server 需求大幅成長，如本中心預估 2026/2027 年 ASIC 晶片量年增 91%/88%；2) Server 單位
DRAM Content 持續提升；3) AI Inference/Agentic AI 帶動 DRAM 儲存需求多樣化，如擴大導入
LPDDR(SOCAMM)於 Server 架構中，其中預估 2027 年 Nvidia VR200 機櫃對應 CPU 搭配的
SOCAMM2 位元需求成長高達 6 倍。

預期 2027 年 HBM 整體市場規模年增 243%，其中位元出貨量年增 55%、ASP 翻倍成長，主因：1) 
HBM 先前因年度議價機制無法反映 2H25 以來 DRAM 漲勢，近期已重新議定 2027 年度合約價；2) 
HBM4 放量出貨，其中 HBM4 佔整體 HBM 產值比重將達 46%。此外，雖 Rubin Ultra 、TPU v9 搭
配的 HBM4E 將由 12Hi 降至 8Hi，但主要為因應供給不足下為確保出貨顆數的應對措施，並非需求
下修。2027 年 HBM 市場份額預期 SK Hynix 約 40~45%、Samsung 約 35~40%、Micron 約
25~30%，SK Hynix 仍為 HBM 龍頭，但因 Samsung HBM4 進度優於同業、SK Hynix 持續調整
HBM4 效能，使 Samsung 市佔率具提升空間。

資料來源：元大投顧預估 資料來源：元大投顧預估 
以 BOM Cost 分布來看，DRAM/NAND 漲價對於 General Server 與 PC 價格之影響程度較高，如
DRAM/NAND 漲 10%，對於 AI Server/ General Server/PC/Smartphone 整機價格之影響分別為
1%/5.3%/3~3.6%/2~3%。 
而組裝廠更改記憶體規格之彈性來看，PC 最高、Server 其次、AI Server 接續、Smartphone 最低。
其中 PC 品牌廠因各地所需之規格不同且主機板可調整彈性相對手機高，加上每年都會針對原有的
零組件對供應商要求降價，因此可以最快調整其記憶體設計以因應記憶體價格變化，因此將較快出
現產品降規之情況；Server 部分以 Storage Server 所受之影響最大，尤其對於以中小企業客戶為
主之白牌業者其價格敏感度較高，不過對 CSP 來說對於 General Server/AI Server( 占比小)漲價之
接受度仍高；而 Smartphone 則只能在下一代產品發布前更改設計，多只能自行吸收或是對下游反
映售價，對於品牌廠毛利與終端需求影響較大。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第9 頁，共 20頁  
2027 年原廠 DRAM/NAND 新增產能有限，仍不足以因應強勁成長的需求 
雖 2027 年中部份新產能將上線，三大 DRAM 國際原廠年底投片月產能預估皆僅較 2026 年底增加
10~15%，長鑫存儲擴產幅度雖達 20~30%仍難以滿足中國國內需求 (目前中國 DRAM 自給率僅
30~40%)，即使搭配製程轉換以提升位元產出，仍 無法滿足本中心預估之全球 DRAM 位元需求量
30%以上的強勁成長，其中國際原廠擴產計畫如下： 
1) Samsung ：P4L 自 3Q25 起陸續投產，預計至 2027 年底月產能將達約 150kwpm，主要用於 1c 
DRAM 以因應強勁的 HBM4 需求，並持續推動現有廠房舊製程轉往 1c nm 以滿足 HBM 與通用型
1c DRAM 市場；此外，P5L Fab 1 已於 2025 年底復工、Fab 2 提前至 2026 年 7 月動工，產能目
標高於現有廠房約 50%以上。 
2) SK Hynix ：M15X 2026 下半年預計投產約 40kwpm，2027 年底月產能將達約 80kwpm，主要用
於 1b 並部分導入 1c DRAM ；公司亦表示 Y1 首個無塵室設備裝機將提前 3 個月至 2027 年 5 月，
以因應強勁的 AI 相關記憶體需求，預計至 2030 年上半年分階段開出共 360kwpm 的產能，Y2 則
預計 2027 年 7 月動工、2029 年 6 月投產，用於生產 HBM 及下世代 DRAM。 
3) Micron ：美國 ID1 預計 2027 年中產出第一批晶圓、ID2 則預計 2028 年底產出；向力積電添購
之銅鑼廠預計提前一季至 2027 年中出貨；新加坡廠區 HBM 封裝產能將於 2027 年上半年開始貢獻。 
4) 長鑫存儲：上海新廠 2027 年開始投產，主要針對 HBM3 等高端記憶體產品，亦新建後段封測廠
以填補中國在 TSV 及堆疊封裝的供應鏈空白，合肥 Phase 3 則預計以 LPDDR4X/5/5X 等產品為主，
預計 2028 年開始投產。 
圖 24：2027 年 DRAM CAPEX 將年增 56% 圖 25：2027 年 NAND CAPEX 將年增 27%

獲利預估與股票評價
正向看待南亞科後續營運，目標價 570 元
展望後續，在 ASP 維持高檔且新廠產能將於 2H27 開始逐步貢獻下，正向看待公司中長期營運成
長，預估 2026/2027 年營收年增 525.5%/66.8%至 4,165.31 億元/6,946.17 億元 ，毛利率年增
60ppts/2ppts 至 82.5%/84.5%，EPS 年增 3457.6%/68.6%至 75.93/128.01 元。
目前公司股價約在 1.8 倍 2027 年每股淨值(BVPS) ( 隱含本益比 3.2 倍)，低於國外同業平均的 2.0
倍，公司過去三年 PB 則落在 0.4~2.8 倍區間。雖目前循環處於 DRAM 報價仍維持高檔但預期後續
季增幅將收斂的階段，然而考量 1) 公司為 DDR4 第一大廠的產業地位且同時具備 DDR5 產品布局、
2) 產品組合將逐步擺脫消費性並持續往 Server/AI 提升(目前相關營收占比已超過 20%)、3) WoW

客製化產品有望帶來更穩定的獲利支撐，有助公司中長期營運更加穩健，以 2.5 倍 PB、2027 年
BVPS 230.7 元，推得目標價 570 元，給予買進評等。

公司簡介  
南亞科技成立於 1995 年，近年於全球 DRAM 市佔率約 1~2%，排名第五，公司目前以 DDR4 為主(佔比 60~70%)，亦具備
DDR5 產品，以應用別而言，PC & Server 約 25~30%、Mobile 約 10~15%、Consumer 約 60~70%。公司生產基地為新北
泰山 3A 廠，月產能 6~6.5 萬片。由於 DDR4/5 為長期主流市場，公司將陸續發展 1B/1C/1D 製程，且預計 2026~2029 年
投入新台幣 3,466 億元建置 5A 新廠產能，預估新產能將於 2H27~2028 年開出。

### Page 17

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：記憶體 2026 年 10 月 27 日 Nanya Tech 第17 頁，共 20頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 南亞科整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於中等風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排行在略為領先的位
置，但在半導體設計與製造的公司中排名領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 南亞科的整體曝險屬於中等水準，與半導體設計與製造行業的平均水準相當。公司較需要改善
的 ESG 議題包含商業道德以及人力資源等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：南亞科在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬較佳。ESG 報告體系完善，公
司已指定董事會成員負責監督 ESG 相關事宜。使用 ESG 目標評估高階主管績效；然而，這些目標並未在薪酬政策中明
確規定。儘管如此，公司的檢舉人保護計畫和環境政策均較為完善。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

## Document 089: 261015_daiwa_TSMC

Source PDF: all report pdf/261015_daiwa_TSMC.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: TSMC continued delivering “beat-n-raise” prints for its 
quarterly results and guidance on 15 October. While we raise our forecasts 
to factor in its better topline and pricing trajectory yet muted margin 
expectation on an incremental basis, we see likely lack of near-term 
passion from investors on limited positive surprises from its results call this 
time. That said, we believe AI demand remains strong and sustainable to 
support our Buy (1) call on the stock, though patience may be necessary 
for investors amid the current jittery environment.

What’s the impact: Results intact, guidance mixed. Despite 3Q26 
topline of USD46.7bn topping guidance (USD44.6-45.8bn) at TSMC, its 
3Q26 EPS came in at TWD29.4, only marginally beating our/Bloomberg 
consensus estimates on lower opex and higher non-operating gains from 
long-term investments more than offsetting lower gross margin associated 
with higher depreciation costs on dilutive N2 ramp (table on p.2). Guidance 
calls for higher topline for 4Q26 with midpoint of the range 6% above our 
previous forecast, thanks to continued AI strength as customers continue to 
increase demand. Yet margins at the operating level looked muted without 
further operating leverage being expected by the street, due to the margin-
dilutive N2 ramp. We raise our 2026-28E forecasts by 3-10% to factor in 
the better topline trajectory, plus our view of better pricing power.

Lack of near-term passion? Apart from the upbeat topline but muted 
margin guidance on an incremental basis, we left the results call with 
limited positive surprises this time which may not drive any passion for 
investors in the near term to catalyse the stock. That said, fundamentals 
remain strong since AI demand continues to mushroom from accelerators 
to CPU and other companion chips, as language models transform from 
generative to agentic for AI assistance. TSMC implied upside risk to its 
long-term AI accelerator revenue CAGR of mid-to-high-50% over 2024-29 
and, as flagged in our Asian Foundry report, we saw upside risk to TSMC’s 
pricing power for 2027, given the still meaningful supply constraint where 
management reiterated its strong conviction in this multi-year AI megatrend 
and active capacity build aimed to narrow the supply/demand gap.

What we recommend: We raise our 12M TP for TSMC to TWD3,200 
(previous: TWD3,000), based on our finetuned ROE-adjusted PBR target of 
7.5x one-year forward BVPS forecast (previous: 7.7x). We reiterate our Buy 
(1) call with key downside risks of “silicon inflation” and geopolitical 
overhangs leading to global inflation and AI capex cut.

How we differ: Our 2028E EPS is 11% below the Bloomberg consensus 
likely on our view calling for a soft-landing in the chip cycle.

15 October 2026

Lack of passion despite strong AI?

➢ Another “beat-n-raise” in quarterly prints but likely … 
➢ … short of catalysts on limited positive surprise this time 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call with a higher 12M TP of TWD3,200

Company profile 
Incorporated in Taiwan in 1987, Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co. (TSMC) is the world’s largest 
complementary metal oxide semiconductor (CMOS) foundry in revenue terms. TSMC offers foundry 
services such as wafer masking, fabrication, probing, packaging and testing, to a high variety of 
customers including fabless chipmakers and IDMs. Its manufacturing fabs are located in Taiwan, China, 
the US and Singapore.

### Page 5

5

Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing (2330 TT): 15 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 15 Oct 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
TSMC's board of directors (BoD) has 10 members, including 6 independent directors
representing 60% of total directors. The background of TSMC's BoD is diversified, with members
from industries, academia, and law. Also, its board members include representatives from not
only Taiwan but also Europe and the US, bringing their experience in operating international
businesses to the board.
We believe the high percentage of independent directors and a diverse mix of expertise from the
board members strengthen the functionality of its BoD, as evidenced by TSMC's leading position
in the foundry market.
Capital management 1
TSMC pays its dividend on a quarterly basis. In the past 5 years, it has managed its dividend
payout well, as evidenced by its dividend in dollar terms, where it has never lowered its dividend.
As the leader in advanced technology nodes, TSMC has kept expanding its capacity for
advanced nodes; as such, we expect its capital intensity to increase to the level near or above
40% over 2025-26 (vs. its long-term target of mid-30%). However, we believe TSMC will at least
sustain its cash dividend payout in dollar terms over 2025-26E. We see its balance between
investment and dividend as appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 1
TSMC's sales to related parties and purchases from related parties remain immaterial as a
percentage of total revenue and cost of goods sold in 2026. We view these levels as insignificant
and therefore see limited risk to TSMC's earnings, cash flow generation, or corporate
governance.
S Supply chainmanagement 1
As the leader in the global foundry industry, TSMC approaches supply chain management by
focusing on 2 main strategies: sustainability risk management and local supply chain
optimisation. It requires all its suppliers to comply with its "Code with Ethics and Business
Conduct", and to follow regulations on human rights and conflict-free minerals.
In 2019, 100% of its tier-1 suppliers signed the "Supplier Code of Conduct" and self-assessment
questionnaire on sustainable management. Also, TSMC's raw materials purchased are 100%
Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) conflict-free. It also requires its critical suppliers to
report on the status of sustainability management in their critical upstream supply chains.
Through these measures, we see TSMC reducing its supply chain risk.
E Water & wastewatermanagement 2
TSMC applies internal and external company resources to develop water reclaimation
technology, implements water conservation measures and uses reeclaimed water in the
manufacturing process. For example, on September 19, 2022, TSMC Tainan Science Park
Reclaimed Water Plant started supplying reclaimed water. In 2025, TSMC aims to replace 5% of
water resources with reclaimed water, and continue to cooperate with the government to
complete the 2nd water reclamation plan in Anping, Tainan. In 2030, TSMC targets to replace >
60% of water resources with reclaimed water.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
TSMC's waste recycling rate reached 96% in 2022 for 8 years in a row. Also, the percentage of
waste sent to landfills was less than 1% for 13 years in a row in 2022.
TSMC achieved reducing its outsourced unit waste disposal (kg/12" equivalent wafer mask
layer) to 0.99 in 2022. Furthermore, TSMC has set a goal to reduce its outsourced unit waste
disposal per wafer to no more than 0.5 in 2030.
E GHG emissions 1
TSMC has continued its efforts to reduce greenhouse gas (GHG) emissions to drive low-carbon
manufacturing. In 2022, TSMC achieved net zero emissions of direct and indirect (from
purchased energy) GHG Emissions in overseas factories, and 100% use of
carbon-neutral natural gas in Taiwan fabs. It has set a target to achieve Net Zero Emissions by
2050.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

動態更新 IC製造 ‧ 台灣

台積電 
(2330.TW /2330 TT)

多、更多、再更多的 N3 產能擴張 
重要訊息 
台積電 3Q26 財報表現符合預期，管理層預估 2026 年營收將年增 44%。 
評論及分析  
3Q26 符合預期，4Q26 展望強勁。3Q26 毛利率 66.4%，受 N2 量產爬坡稀
釋影響，較我們預估低 1.6 個百分點，但符合公司財測。受惠營收成長及營
業費用控管，營業利益及營益率均符合預期。台積電 4Q26 營收財測為 523
億至 543 億美元，以中間值計算季增 14%，較我們先前預估高出 7%，毛
利率及營益率則預期持平，主要受惠 HPC 需求強勁及 N2 量產爬坡。 
N3 持續擴產，並評估美國亞利桑那州以外的擴產機會 。隨 4Q26 展望轉
強，台積電將 2026 年美元營收成長預估由原先 40%以上上調至 44%，並
維持全年資本支出 600 億至 640 億美元。台積電過去鮮少將單一製程產能
擴充至超過原先規劃的目標( 約每月 15 萬片)。然而，除近期宣布的三座晶圓
廠(台南 Fab 18 、亞利桑那州二廠及熊本二廠) 外，台積電再宣布於台南新增
一座 N3 晶圓廠，預計於 1H28 量產。整體而言，我們預估 N3 產能將由
2026 年底的每月 15 萬至 16 萬片，在未來兩年進一步增加 8 萬片，增幅達
50%。我們認為此次擴產反映三項需求：(1)Agentic AI 帶動的 CPU 需求；
(2)HBM4e 客製化基底晶粒；以及(3)AI ASIC 。此外，台積電亦正評估在美國
亞利桑那州以外地區擴產的可能性，例如德州，但目前尚未做出最終決定。  
不會將任何商機拱手讓給競爭對手 。管理層多次強調不會讓競爭對手取得任
何商機，體現在 N3 產能的積極擴充。此外，儘管我們預期 Google(美 )委託
Intel(美 )生產的 Humufish 目前沒有備援計劃。然而，在必要情況下，台積電
將協助客戶確保產品如期交付， 暗示若 Intel 的 EMIB-T 製程進度落後，台積
電有望進一步提升市占率。部分投資人可能擔憂台積電導入 ASML High -NA
設備的時程較競爭對手晚兩年，進而影響其技術領先地位。但我們持不同看
法，採用最新設備並不必然代表技術領先。若 在 2030 年前台積電能在不投
資昂貴設備的情況下達到相近的製程效能，反而 有望取得相較同業更顯著的
成本優勢。 
投資建議  
儘管 3Q26 財報符合預期，且我們預估 4Q26 表現將更為強勁，但在市場預
期極高下，投資人仍可能對短期毛利率走勢有所疑慮。考量未來的成長動能
及穩健的執行能力，我們建議投資人趁股價回檔時逢低布局。我們僅微幅調
整獲利預估， 但隨評價基準遞延至 2028 年，我們 將目標價上調至 3,650
元，仍基於景氣上行週期平均的 20 倍本益比，維持「增加持股」評等 。 
投資風險  
全球景氣不確定性；積極擴產導致供給過剩。  
增加持股‧維持 
  收盤價 October 15 (NT$)   2,440

法說會重點數據 
 4Q26 財測： 營收預估為 523 億至 543 億美元，以中間值計算季增
14%；毛利率 65-67%，營益率 56.5- 58.5%。 
 2026 年展望：全年營收成長預估上調至年增 44%，維持資本支出 600
億至 640 億美元。  
 毛利率稀釋：海外擴產預計稀釋 2026 年毛利率 2 個百分點，未來幾年
稀釋幅度將擴大至 3-4 個百分點。  
 產能規劃：  
 N2 製程家族產能預計於 2027 年增加至兩倍以上。  
 台南新增 N3 晶圓廠預計於 1H27 量產，另一座 N3 晶圓廠則規劃於
1H28 量產。 
 亞利桑那州 N3 二廠已完成廠房建設，目前正進行設備搬入及安
裝，預計於 3Q27 量產，略早於原訂時程。  
 日本 N3 製程預計於 2028 年量產。 
 A14 製程已開始進行產能準備，預計於 2027 年如期試產，並於
2028 年量產。

ESG 圖表定義  
項目 定義 說明 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括
為滿足能源使用而生產的電力(公用事業為出售而生產) 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下

購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者(太陽能、風能、水能、生
物質能、地熱能)之量 
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源

再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
CO2約當排放量 
直接 CO2與 CO2約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量(範圍 1排放量) 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳(CO2)、甲烷(CH4)、一氧化二氮(N2O)、氫氟碳化物(HFCS)、全氟化合物
(PFCS)、六氟化硫(SF6)、三氟化氮(NF3)

CO2約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2與 CO2約當排放量(公噸)銷售(百萬元)比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量(範圍 1排放量) 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳(CO2)、甲烷(CH4)、一氧化二氮(N2O)、氫氟碳化物(HFCS)、全氟化合物
(PFCS)、六氟化硫(SF6)、三氟化氮(NF3)

廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄
物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內

廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物

總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮

環保支出 
環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處
置、處理、衛生、清理支出

員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因(自願或非自願)離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失
能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 = (離開之員工/平均員工人數)*100 
- 平均員工人數 = (本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數)/2 
- 本財年末員工數 = 上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數

女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100

女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100

教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練(如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展) 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1天 = 8小時工作

每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數  
資料來源：Refinitiv，凱基

Upward earnings revisions; reiterate our Buy on both names — We raise EMC’s
2026/27 /28E earnings by 20%/31%/44% to factor in price hikes and enhanced
product mix. We thus raise EMC’s TP to NT$7,400 (28x 2027E EPS) from NT$6,000
(30x 2027E EPS). For TUC, we raise 2026/27 /28E earnings by 21%/29%/27% to
factor in price hike and enhanced product mix as well. We lift our TUC TP to
NT$2,300 (23x 2027E EPS) from NT$1,950 (25x 2027E EPS). We lower PE multiple

for both names given recent AI concerns amid the rate hike backdrop.

Elite Material
Company description
Established in 1992 in Taoyuan, Taiwan, EMC is a global leading copper clad
laminate (CCL) and prepreg supplier for HDI and smartphone applications.
EMC has developed several green materials products, from Mid Loss, Low
Loss, Very Low Loss, Ultra Low Loss down to Extreme Low Loss categories. In
the past few years, EMC has continued to gain presence in AI datacenter,

networking and HPC sectors. In the AI server market, EMC is one of the key
suppliers to global AI GPU customers and a major supplier to US CSPs for AI
ASIC projects.
Investment strategy
We are positive over EMC’s long-term growth outlook as we believe its
technology leadership in high-end CCL should position it well to benefit from
the structural spec migration in PCB materials amid the AI demand boom. We
expect EMC to maintain its market share in AI GPU projects and to gradually

gain share in next-gen ASIC projects in 2026. Given its leading position in
high-end CCL, sufficient raw material support, expanding capacity and solid
earnings outlook, we rate EMC a Buy.
Valuation
Our target price for EMC is set at NT$7,400, based on a target PE multiple of
28x on our 2027E EPS average. We believe our target PE multiple, at the peak
of its forward PE average in the past 5 years, is justified by a strong earnings
outlook supported by its leading position in CCL supply for AI GPU and ASIC

(high m/s) from 2026E onwards. We also believe the continued spec upgrade
for CCL, price hikes and product mix enhancement should help support its
margin expansion going forward. At our target price, the shares would trade at
55x/28x 2026E/27E PB.
Risks
Citi’s quant system rates EMC high-risk given high share price volatility, which
we largely attribute to frequent debates over the spec upgrade for next-gen AI
servers. In general, we believe some investors initially tend to have high hope

for aggressive spec upgrade without sufficiently considering the scalability
and cost structure of CCL products, which then leads to a shortfall in the end.
However, from a longer-term perspective, we see a decent and clear spec
upgrade trend with meaningful ASP growth potential among AI servers of
different generations. As such, we do not assign a high-risk rating.
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) a slower-than-expected CCL upgrade trend; 2) weaker-than-

expected AI server demand; 3) production bottlenecks in AI supply chain (eg,
glass, copper foil, foundry or OSAT); 4) unexpected share loss in key AI server
projects; and 5) stricter practice of OOC policy by end customers.
TUC
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Taiwan PCB & Laminates

13
Company description
TUC was established in 1974 and mainly produced optical glass initially. Since
1997, it started to provide copper clad laminate (CCL) and prepreg.
Headquartered in Hsinchu, Taiwan, TUC has an extensive global service
network spanning mainland China, Japan, Korea, the US and Germany. It has
been specializing in high-speed CCL for networking and now has a strong
position in supplying 800G switch opportunities. The company also started

to supply AI ASIC from 2025.
Investment strategy
We expect increasing AI ASIC exposure for TUC as it continues to see new
project opportunities among US CSP customers, including the high-end CCL
market for Trainium chip. We like TUC as it has production expertise in high-
speed CCL products, especially in networking, such as 800G, and has long
built solid relationships with upstream high-end materials suppliers to ensure
raw material support. We rate TUC a Buy given meaningful 800G ramp from

2026E onwards and its increasing opportunities in AI projects within CSPs
and enterprise players.
Valuation
Our target price for TUC is set at NT$2,300, based on a target PE multiple of
23x on our 2027E EPS. We believe our target PE multiple, at the peak of TUC’s
PE average in the last upcycle, is justified by its strong margin expansion
potential on a higher sales mix from AI ASIC server/800G products and price
hikes on supply tightness. We believe TUC’s growth outlook is well supported

by AI ASIC and 800G with its capacity ramp in Thailand from 3Q26 onward.
At our target price, the shares would trade at 2026E/27E PB of 53x/23x.
Risks
Citi’s quant system rates TUC High Risk given high share price volatility, which
we largely attribute to frequent debates over the spec upgrade for next-gen AI
servers. In general, we believe some investors initially tend to have high hope
for aggressive spec upgrade without sufficiently considering the scalability

and cost structure of CCL products, which then leads to a shortfall in the end.
However, from a longer-term perspective, we see a decent and clear spec
upgrade trend with meaningful ASP growth potential among AI servers of
different generations. As such, we do not assign a High Risk rating.
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) a slower-than-expected CCL upgrade trend; 2) weaker-than-
expected AI server/800G demand; 3) production bottlenecks in AI supply

chain (e.g., glass, copper foil, foundry, or OSAT); 4) unexpected share loss in
key AI server projects; 5) flexible practice of OOC policy by end customers;
and 6) slow development of the PCB industry in Southeast Asia.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.
Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk

rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Update
Accton Technology Corporation  | Asia Pacific
Read-across from Celestica's
3Q26 Results
We see positive implications for Accton given demand strength
(1.6T migration amid sustaining 800G) and breadth (scale up
opportunities) of ethernet  switches in future.
Celestica (CLS.N, not covered) 3Q26 results:
• 3Q26 revenue of US$5,830mn (+24% QoQ/+83% YoY), non-GAAP operating
margin of 8.5%,  a record high, and non-GAAP EPS of US$3.30 (+30% QoQ/

+109% YoY).
° Higher than guidance and 7%/13% above consensus for revenue/earnings,
respectively.
° Drivers: Stronger CCS revenue (84% of the mix in 3Q26), including
networking and compute/storage growth of 68% and 242% YoY,
respectively, vs. guidance 60% and 190% YoY.
• 4Q26 revenue guided at US$6,275-6,575mn (+10% QoQ/+76% YoY at the
midpoint) vs. consensus at US$6,396mn, management raising 2026 target to
US$21bn for revenue (+69% YoY vs. prior US$20.5bn) and US$11.55 for non-

GAAP EPS  (+91% YoY vs. prior US$11.3).
° Driven by hyperscaler-led 800G demand and ramping 1.6T programs,
along with continued AI compute and storage demand.
• 2027 revenue guided at US$40bn (+90% YoY), non-GAAP OPM at 9.0%
(+0.6ppt YoY) and non-GAAP EPS at US$21.5 (+86% YoY)
° It expects CCS to grow 110% (1.6T/800G on networking and ASIC ramp
on compute/storage) and  ATS at 10%, with revenue skewed to  2H on
multiple large ramps and momentum likely sustaining into  2028.

• Pluggable should remain the mainstream interconnect solution, while CPO
might pick up more meaningfully from 3.2T, per company.
• CLS expects ethernet scale up could represent new opportunities in future.
Our view:
• While the customer profiles are different, we still see positive implications
from Celestica's comments for Accton regarding demand strength (1.6T
migration amid sustaining 800G) and breadth (scale up opportunities) of
ethernet  switches.

• We stay constructive on Accton, and believe   it will continue to benefit from
the secular growth in network switches amid increasing data traffic, spec
upgrades, and a wider customer base as well as the AI accelerator modules
on the initiatives for compute diversity.

More Takeaways
Further comments from Celestica:
• Demand visibility is lengthening and it is now having conversations with customers
for 2029/2030 projects
• Still seeing lots of pluggables during 1.6T, while it expects CPO to play out more
prominently in 3.2T.
• It has won a new hyperscaler program to co-design and manufacture a 1.6T rack-
scale CPO networking solution, with production scheduled to ramp in late 2027,

per management.

### Page 4

M
Update
4
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Accton Technology Corporation (2345.TW)
Base case, P/E, which we believe better captures the company's earnings growth momentum.
We assign a target P/E of 30x to our 2027 EPS estimate, which we view as justified by an
operating profit CAGR of 48% for 2025-28, based on our estimates. This implies a PEG of
around 0.6, vs. its average of 0.7 over 2019-25, when the stock was trading at a P/E of 22x for
an operating profit CAGR of 32%.

Risks to Upside
n Stronger data center capex
n Faster network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand losing share in AI networks
n Stronger custom AI accelerator growth
n Stronger ODM direct business model adoption
Risks to Downside
n Weaker data center capex
n Slower network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand taking more share in AI networks
n Softer custom AI accelerator growth
n Softer ODM direct business model adoption

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 178: 4541晟田 台新

Source PDF: all report pdf/4541晟田 台新.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-20

### Page 1

本刊載之報告為台新投顧於特定日期之分析，已力求陳述內容之可靠性，純屬研究性質，僅作參考，使用者應明瞭內容之時效性，
審慎考量投資風險，並就投資結果自行負責。報告著作權屬台新投顧所有，禁止任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。 
台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113) 金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

晟田(4541)：航太及半導體客戶需求延續，帶動 2027 年獲利成長 
建議：中立轉買進

一、晟田為航太精密機械零件加工廠 
晟田成立於 1991 年 8 月，實收資本額 6.76 億元，截至
2Q26 底，員工約 686 位。主要從事航太零組件(包括發
動機機匣與懸塊、起落架)及機械零組件(包括半導體真
空泵浦零組件、食品飲料填充包裝設備零組件、工業自
動化精密傳動零組件等產品)之製造加工與銷售等業務。
2025 年前五大客戶為漢翔(2634)、日本 E 客戶、
SLS(Safran Landing Systems)、SPP (6355.JP)及長榮
航太(2645)。2026 年前 9 個月產品別營收佔比為航太零
組件(客供料) 48%，航太零組件(自購料) 25%，半導體
設備零組件 23%，其他 4%。

二 、 夏季電價及費用認列影響獲利 ， 預估3Q26 稅後 EPS 
1.26 元 
晟田 3Q26 營收 5.6 億元(+1.9%QoQ，+30.9%YoY)，
季增主因為受惠航太客供料客戶拉貨動能成長及半導體
客戶需求提升。獲利方面，儘管產品組合持續優化，惟
受到夏季電價及籌資相關費用認列影響，預估營業利益
1.01 億元(-6%QoQ，+97.1%YoY)。假設匯率持穩，預
估稅後淨利 0.85 億元(-1%QoQ，+34.4%YoY)， 稅後EPS 
1.26 元。 
三、產品組合持續優化，預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 1.3 元 
展望 4Q26，半導體方面，考量客戶需求持續強勁，有
望帶動出貨動能；航太方面，波音(BA.US) 737 系列機
型月產能有望於 2026 年底自 42 架提升至 47 架，另，
根據空中巴士(EADSY.US)規劃，2026 年預計交付 870
架，截至 Sep-26 交付達成率約 6 成，預期 4Q26 將加
速交機 ， 帶動航太零組件拉貨需求 。 整體而言 ， 預估4Q26
營收 5.62 億元(+0.5%QoQ，+20.7%YoY)。獲利方面，
隨產品組合持續優化，且無電價因素干擾，預估營業利
益 1.05 億元(+3.9%QoQ，+28.6%YoY)。 假設匯率持穩，
預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 1.3 元。 
四、需求延續，預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 4.51 元 
展望後市，儘管航太供應鏈仍受原物料短缺、人力及產
能瓶頸影響，惟波音及空巴在手訂單仍維持高位，顯示
終端需求具支撐。隨供應鏈瓶頸逐步緩解，預期將帶動
航太零組件拉貨需求回升，有利晟田營運成長。半導體
方面，受惠終端客戶持續擴大資本支出，帶動相關產品
需求穩定成長。整體而言，預估 2026 年營收 21.2 億元
(+29.1%YoY)。獲利方面，隨產品組合持續優化，預估
毛利率 25.1%，稅後淨利 3.1 億元(+122.9%YoY)，稅後
EPS 4.51 元。 
展望 2027 年，考量航空引擎零組件客戶需求強勁，晟
田規劃擴充相關產能，新廠有望於 2H27 投產；半導體
方面，持續優化相關產能，以因應客戶需求成長。整體
而言，預估 2027 年營收 23.5 億元(+10.7%YoY)。獲利
方面，考量新廠投產後折舊費用增加，預估毛利率年減
至 24.8%， 稅後淨利3.3 億元(+9%YoY)， 稅後EPS 4.58
元(以增資稀釋後股本計算)。 
五、中立轉買進理由 
評價方面，以 2027 年 EPS 估算，目前 PE 約為 13X，
介於晟田歷史區間 12X~20X 下緣 ， 調升至買進評等 ， 目
標價 75 元(2026F PE 17X)。

## Document 232: [000270] 기아(000270) 잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기기아(000270) 잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000270] 기아(000270) 잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기기아(000270) 잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.15  
잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기

3Q26 Preview 
기아는 3Q26E 매출액 31.1조원(+8.2% YoY, -6.0% QoQ), 영업이익 2.2조원
(+53.1% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 7.2%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 
물량은 좋았으나, 아쉬운 믹스와 환율 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.8% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.0만대
(+8.6%)를 기록했다. EV·HEV 확대에 따른 ASP 상승은 지속되고 있다. 전쟁으로 인
한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 재료비 부담은 전분기 대비 3,000억~4,000억원 증가할 
것으로 예상된다. EV 판매 비중은 2분기 13%에서 3분기 15%, 유럽은 35%에서 
43%로 확대되며 유럽 내 마진 희석은 불가피하다. 원화 강세에 따른 영업상 부담은 
판매보증충당부채 환입 등으로 상쇄돼 3분기 환율 영향은 제한적일 전망이다. 판매보
증충당부채 환입은 QoQ +5,000억원 수준으로 추정된다. 4분기부터 환율 효과는 부정
적일 것이며, 환율 10원 변동 시 연간 손익 민감도는 약 1,500억원 수준이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 165,000원으로 하향 
미국 조지아 공장의 텔루라이드 HEV(2.8만대)와 HMGMA의 스포티지 HEV(1.0만대) 
신규 생산으로 북미의 견조한 HEV 수요에 대응하며, 2027년 믹스 개선이 기대된다. 
유럽은 EV3·EV5로 점유율을 확대하는 가운데 유럽 내 할인율 하락과 EV 잔존가치 
회복으로 판매 여건이 개선되고 있다. 향후 IAA 규제에 따른 법인·플릿 혜택 제한은 
중국 업체의 원가 우위를 줄일 수 있을 전망이다. 
원화 약세 지속을 반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다.  
다만, 현재 주가는 12M Fwd P/E 기준 4.8배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 
수준, 저가 매수 기회라 판단된다. 현재 배당 수익률은 6% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높은 
배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

목표주가 (하향) 165,000 원 
현재주가 108,600 원 
상승여력 51.9 %

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
• Central banks’ communication is carefully scrutinised for
every nuance. Lately, there seems to have been more RBA
communication to pore over. The method of delivery has
also evolved. This has sometimes caused confusion when
tone seems to vary. Different audiences call for different

tone and varying complexity.
• The content has evolved, too. RBA officials are increasingly
using speeches directed to professional audiences to
discuss more technical estimates of inferred variables such
as neutral rates and financial conditions. They are also
highlighting that monetary policy combats inflation in more
ways than just squeezing mortgage holders.
• One thing has not changed: the RBA is not in the business
of providing ‘forward guidance’ promises about the future

path of interest rates. It does, however, give more running
commentary about how it is interpreting recent data. These
interpretations are themselves useful signals about what
the RBA thinks it might do.
Market participants pore over every utterance by central banks
for clues to future rate moves. They will have plenty to pore over
in the run-up to the November RBA decision. After the flurry
of fireside chats this week, ’60 Minutes’ will run a pre-recorded

interview with chief economist Sarah Hunter on Sunday. Next
Wednesday, the whole senior policy team will appear before
Senate Estimates, just as the quarterly CPI is released. (This
timing will have been the Senate’s choice, not the RBA’s.) After
the decision will come the Statement on Monetary Policy, a
media release and press conference. More speeches will follow,
including one by the Deputy Governor on the Thursday.
RBA communication does seem to have changed, not just

following the RBA Review but also more recently. There may
be fewer policy meetings each year, but the communication
outside that certainly feels more frequent than a couple of years
ago. Back in 2024, speaking engagements felt a little sparse
outside the post-meeting media conference and parliamentary
appearances. We have counted 58 public engagements so
far this year on the Speeches page on the RBA’s website.
This compares with 58 for the whole of last year and 52 in

2024. Excluding the ‘compulsory’ press conferences and
parliamentary appearances, fewer than half of the 2024 events
were related to economics, markets and monetary policy.
The ‘how’ has changed
The way the RBA communicates with markets has changed
as well. There are fewer set-piece speeches on policy and a
Open mouth operations
Luci Ellis
Chief Economist, Westpac Group
lot more semi-scripted ‘fireside chats’. Compared with a few
years ago, there are more interviews across a wide range of

media. This is a big change from the pre-Review world. It was
a huge deal when the previous Governor appeared on the
7:30 Report ; it is hard to imagine him agreeing to an Assistant
Governor appearing on 60 Minutes.
Part of the change reflects increased efforts to reach a general
audience. Central banks want to explain why they do what
they do. This matters especially when interest rates are rising
but the household sector is not the source of the inflation.

Speaking differently to different audiences can sometimes
confuse markets. As the RBA’s Chief Communication Officer
highlighted in a recent panel discussion, the post-meeting
media conference is designed to speak to the public. Market
participants and economists certainly tune in, but we are not
the primary audience. So, if you think that what the Governor
said in the media conference sounded more ‘dovish’ than
what another official says in a more market-oriented forum,

it is partly because she is trying to convey that the RBA
understands that its recent policy actions are making things
harder for many Australians. But this is in much the same way
that a doctor might say, “This might hurt a bit.” It does, but that
will not stop the treatment.
A related issue is that it is easier to control tone in pre-drafted
material such as the minutes and set-piece speeches than in
unstructured Q&A. Senior central bankers are trained to be

careful with their words in unscripted settings. If the tone
differs, the minutes are still more likely to say what the Board
really means than an off-the-cuff answer.
Greater communication with the broader public might have
reduced the pressure to make the set-piece speeches more
accessible. Research on central bank communication often
uses measures such as the Flesch–Kincaid Grade Level and
Flesch Reading Ease score. A comprehensive analysis was

beyond the scope of this note. However, a spot comparison
suggests four speeches from 2026 ( 1, 2, 3, 4) were harder
to read than four from 2021 (1, 2, 3, 4). The 2021 speeches
scored in the 11 to 12 range, which is high-school-level
“The RBA does not want to promise what it will
do, but it is increasingly trying to tell us what
it thinks. "

### Page 3

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
complexity. The 2026 speeches scored between 12 and
15, which is academic or technically dense text requiring
university-level education. (Including two of my own 2021
speeches is an obvious caveat, but the two speeches by then-
Governor Phil Lowe also scored better on these metrics than

the sample from this year. It should be noted, though, that
readability metrics do not necessarily imply better clarity if the
concepts discussed are more abstract.)
The ‘what’ has changed
As noted above, the content of RBA communication has
evolved, too. As its intellectual framework has begun to put
more weight on the balance of supply and demand, and those
invisible ‘star’ variables (NAIRUs, neutral real rates, ‘financial
conditions’, etc.), the communication to professional audiences

has increasingly focused on those topics.
More recently, the RBA has also placed more emphasis on
the full range of transmission channels from interest rates to
inflation (though not, as we have noted previously, the labour
supply channel). This is understandable in a world of supply
shocks. People need to understand that monetary policy is not
simply an exercise in hitting mortgage borrowers. We doubt
they would be comforted, though, by the Deputy Governor’s

explanation this week that the ‘cashflow channel’ was the
smallest of the transmission channels from interest rates
to inflation, with the exchange rate much more prominent.
That implies a lot of pain for a small group, for little effect on
inflation. It raises the question of whether other policy tools
or institutional arrangements might get the same effect on
inflation through the other channels without hitting a minority
of households disproportionately.

We have also seen a move away from using catchphrases
to summarise the position. From ‘narrow path’ to ‘not
ruling anything in or out’ to ‘cautious and predictable’, once
entrenched, a phrase can outlast its usefulness.
One thing that has not changed is the way the minutes record
the presentations to the Board and the members’ discussion. The
minutes rarely convey much new information beyond the post-
meeting statements. Sometimes, though, the minutes reveal the

Board’s reaction to the staff’s analysis. The August 2024 minutes
were a good example: the discussion highlighted just how
sensitive the forecasts were to key judgements by the staff.
The ‘why’ has not changed
Despite these changes, the underlying objectives of the RBA’s
communication have not changed. It wants to explain – and
perhaps justify – its decisions. And it wants to describe how
it interprets incoming data, especially inflation, the labour

market and the balance of supply and demand.
The RBA, like any central bank, is not really in the business
of providing ‘forward guidance’ about future moves.
Circumstances change, so it can never guarantee a particular
future path for interest rates. Some academic literature argues
that promising to keep rates low for a long time (or high,
for that matter) independently amplifies the transmission
of monetary policy beyond the effect of current-period

interest rates alone, by shifting the short end of the yield
curve. Central banks including the RBA have certainly tried
jawboning in this way from time to time. The prevailing view,
though, is that the potential cost of reputational loss if things
do not turn out as predicted outweighs the benefits of a
slightly stronger transmission mechanism.
In this context, it is the interpretation of data that provides
the signal about future policy directions. This is why it was

noteworthy that the Governor still characterised the labour
market as ‘a little tight’ after the September meeting, even
though the Board’s post-meeting communication did not. We
have been noting for some time that the RBA staff have a
downbeat view of supply capacity. Together with the RBA’s
oft-stated concerns about inflation, this is why the Deputy
Governor’s ‘hawkishness’ did not surprise us.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: managing expectations
While annual retail sales growth improved in September,
rising from 0.4%yr to 1.1%yr, the current pace of growth
is only a quarter of the gain seen just a year ago and very
weak versus history. Consumer spending continues to be

constrained by weak confidence, particularly with respect to
employment prospects and wealth. On housing wealth, five
years into the property downturn, house prices continue to
decline month-on-month and investment remains in a deep
downturn, –20.6%ytd.
Total fixed asset investment has been detracting from
annual GDP growth since April owing to a lull in high-tech
investment, a lack of confidence in future demand amongst
domestically oriented businesses as well as property’s

malaise. For aggregate growth to hold up through 2027 and
2028 as the contribution from net exports moderates, broad-
based stimulus in scale will prove necessary. Expect recent
targeted support measures to be broadened and given much
greater scale in coming months.
Finally, in the UK, inflation accelerated from 3.1%yr to 3.5%yr
in September, exceeding both market expectations and the
Bank of England's July forecast. The surprise was largely
driven by energy, fuel prices rising 5.4%mth. However, core

inflation also edged up from 2.6%yr to 2.8%yr as services
inflation increased from 3.4%yr to 3.7%yr. While not an
immediate concern inflation, the labour market showing
resilience, the ILO unemployment rate falling from 4.9% to
4.7% in August, suggests demand risks for inflation will need
close monitoring as well as supply-side concerns.
Next week will see the focus once again return to the US as
the FOMC meet and US Q3 GDP is released. For both events,

the implications for risks to the outlook will be the focus. The
week after, the US mid-term elections are due.
With little domestic data of significance, participants’
attention focused on RBA Deputy Governor Hauser's fireside
chat, which saw an abrupt shift in market expectations of a
follow-up rate hike at the November meeting.
Hauser warned in his remarks that the current inflation outlook
was "materially worse" than the RBA's August forecasts.

Three upside inflation risks were characterised as having
"crystallised" ahead of the September meeting – the Middle
East, the AI boom, and domestic supply constraints. Hauser
noted the Middle East picture "looks worse" given oil prices
are "about a third higher than [the RBA] assumed in August”.
He then positioned AI and data centre developments among
an "extraordinary sequence of positive demand shocks around
the world", with "higher AI prices, tech prices feeding through

into CPI". Finally, on domestic capacity pressures, the Deputy
Governor noted that "inflation's been coming in at or a little
bit above expectations, despite an apparent easing in the
labour market". He went on to state that the staff are currently
assessing "whether [the RBA] need to change [its] judgement
on capacity pressures in the labour market again" – in other
words, revise up their assumption for the NAIRU.
Hauser's comments prompted a material reassessment of

market pricing for a follow-up November hike, from just a
20% chance at the end of last week to a 50/50 proposition
today ahead of next week's Q3 CPI. For that release, we are
forecasting a 1.3% (4.1%yr) lift in headline inflation and a
0.9 (3.5%yr) increase in trimmed mean inflation. Our preview
provides more detail behind the component mix for Q3 as
well as our upward revisions to the preliminary Q4 forecast.
Provided inflation prints broadly in line with our expectations,

the RBA is likely to hike by 25bp at the November meeting.
Across in New Zealand, consumer prices rose 0.7% in Q3
and annual inflation eased from 4.1%yr to 3.7%yr. Under
the surface of this ‘as expected’ read though, several closely
watched measures of core inflation took a step higher to now
be at or slightly above the top of the RBNZ’s target band. Our
NZ economics team consequently expect the RBNZ will raise
the OCR 25bps to 3.00% at its October policy review, while

retaining a 4.00% terminal OCR forecast for September 2027.
Earlier in the week, China's economy surprised  to the upside in
Q3, GDP growth accelerating from 0.9%qtr to 1.2%qtr, lifting
annual growth from 4.3%yr to 4.5%yr – keeping authorities on
track to achieve their 4.5–5.0% full-year growth objective.
Elliot Clarke, Head of International Economics
Ryan Wells, Economist
Illiana Jain, Economist
“With little domestic data of significance,
participants’ attention focused on RBA Deputy

Governor Hauser's fireside chat. Offshore,
China GDP was the key release. "

5
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
RBNZ now expected to hike in October
We now expect the RBNZ will deliver a 25bp hike at its
policy review on 28 October. While September quarter
inflation was slightly below the RBNZ’s forecast, the
underlying detail was firm. More recently, increases in

fuel prices point to a material lift in inflation by year-end.
We expect further interest rate increases over the coming
months, with the OCR reaching a peak of 4% next year.
Softer headline inflation but firm core pressures
leave RBNZ on course to hike in October
We now expect the RBNZ to raise the Official Cash Rate
by 25bp at its October policy review (previously, we had
expected a pause at this meeting). This change in view reflects
significant upside risks to near-term inflation relative to the

RBNZ’s previous forecast.
While in line with our forecast, the September quarter inflation
figures were slightly softer than the RBNZ expected, with prices
rising 3.7% over the past year (compared with the RBNZ’s
forecast of 3.9%). However, the details were less reassuring.
Excluding volatile items such as food and fuel, underlying
inflation pressures remained firm. Closely watched measures
of core inflation moved higher and now sit close to, or a little

above, the top of the RBNZ’s target band. We also saw an
increase in the number of goods recording price rises, taking
that measure back to levels seen in the wake of the pandemic.
Alongside the existing firmness in underlying inflation, the
risks for inflation over the coming quarters are firmly tilted to
the upside. That near-term upside risk has also been noted by
the Governor. Following a sharp rise in global fuel costs, we
expect inflation to reach 4.3% by the end of this year (above

the RBNZ’s September MPS forecast for +3.9%). While that is
mainly due to higher prices at the pump, businesses in sectors
such as construction are also reporting related increases in
transport and materials costs. Consistent with those trends,
we expect core inflation to remain in the upper part of the
RBNZ’s target band through 2027.
Reinforcing the case for an OCR hike at the October meeting,
the New Zealand dollar is notably weaker than the RBNZ

assumed: on a trade-weighted basis, the NZD is currently
3.6% below the RBNZ’s September projections. If sustained,
that would add to inflation pressures over the next few
quarters. We suspect this will not have shifted the RBNZ’s
medium-term outlook for inflation.
Several other developments need to be weighed against that
firmer inflation outlook. First, wholesale long-term interest rates
have risen markedly, and there has also been a more moderate

rise in some mortgage rates. The RBNZ is likely to note the role of
those increases in managing medium-term inflation risks.
In addition, the RBNZ is likely to continue to emphasise that
there is little evidence of a lift in businesses’ longer-term plans
for price setting at this stage in response to the rise in energy
prices (sometimes referred to as “second-round inflation
effects”). We expect the RBNZ to highlight the risk that more
persistent inflation pressures could emerge, depending on the

duration of the oil price shock.
We also expect the RBNZ to note that the labour market
remains cyclically weak and is not a significant driver of inflation.
Employment growth that remains weak relative to labour force
growth, subdued growth in job vacancies and low labour market
turnover are consistent with this view and could be emphasised.
Finally, the RBNZ is likely to acknowledge that while the
global growth outlook remains solid, there are significant

downside risks from the Iran war and concerns about the
sustainability of the AI investment cycle.
Balancing those concerns, we think the MPC will lift the
cash rate next week. However, a vote seems very possible as
differences in view will likely remain among the MPC about
how much the medium-term inflation outlook has really
shifted in recent weeks.
In September, the bulk of the MPC saw upside risks to the
inflation outlook while two members (Conway and Hansen)

thought the risks were balanced. Recent developments will
not have made those seeing upside risks to inflation more
Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist

### Page 6

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
6
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
comfortable, while the more dovish MPC members will
likely focus on the ongoing downside risks for growth given
continued softness in house prices and the mounting pressure
on real incomes. Indeed, all MPC members saw some degree
of downside risks to growth which likely remain relevant. All

these messages should remain in the RBNZ’s updated views.
The MPC is likely to continue to present its decision as a
gradual evolution of the approach taken since July: a higher
OCR now provides insurance against the need for a much
higher OCR later if upside risks to medium-term inflation
materialise. We doubt the aim will be to push market interest
rates materially higher.
RBNZ to leave door open for further hikes, OCR
still expected to peak at 4% in 2027

In its forward guidance, the RBNZ is likely to leave open the option
of raising the OCR further at the December Monetary Policy
Statement, without presenting an increase as certain. While the
RBNZ will not publish an updated OCR projection at this review,
the press statement is likely to note that further increases may
be needed, depending on economic conditions. The September
projection showed the OCR eventually rising to 3.25%.
We expect the RBNZ will end up delivering another 25bp

hike in December, followed by a further hike in February.
That would take the OCR to 3.5%, roughly where the RBNZ
estimates the neutral rate to be. At that point, we expect the
RBNZ to slow the pace of rate increases (previously, we had
expected that point to be reached a little later).
We continue to expect further 25bp hikes at the June and
September 2027 meetings, taking the OCR to a peak of 4.0%.
While oil prices are generating significant swings in inflation,

underlying pressures remain firm. More restrictive conditions are
likely to be needed to bring core inflation sustainably back to 2%.
Scenarios
Our central scenario, with a probability of around 60%, is that
the RBNZ increases the OCR by 25bp and signals that a further
increase in December remains an option, without committing to
a markedly tighter future stance than it outlined in September.
Alternative scenarios include:
• A hawkish scenario (around a 20% probability) would see

the OCR increase to 3%, with the RBNZ placing greater
emphasis on upside risks to core and medium-term inflation.
That would suggest an upward revision to the RBNZ’s
projected OCR path in December. Downside risks to the
exchange rate could also be emphasised.
• A dovish scenario (around a 20% probability) would
see the RBNZ leave the OCR unchanged and signal that
the medium-term inflation outlook has not changed
meaningfully from its September forecast, despite higher

near-term inflation. In this scenario, the RBNZ would
reiterate its previous forecast: a 25bp increase in December
and one further hike during 2027.
How might the election play into the decision?
While next week’s meeting is close to the General Election
on 7 November, the decision taken by the MPC will not be
politically motivated. However, the election outcome is close.
And should it result in a Labour Party-led coalition, it seems
likely the new government would adopt the Labour Party’s

promise to restore the RBNZ’s dual mandate. That would
require the MPC to weigh the degree of labour market slack
more highly in its deliberations. As the degree of labour market
slack currently seems high, a new mandate might justify the
MPC taking more time to return inflation to the middle of the
target range (our earlier note discusses this issue). This could
figure into the deliberations of some MPC members if the
decision between leaving the OCR unchanged and raising it

now is viewed as finely balanced.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Sep Private Sector Credit (%mth)
Oct 30, Last: 0.6, Westpac f/c: 0.5
Market f/c: 0.5, Range: 0.4 to 0.6
Private sector credit growth came in slightly firmer than
expected at 0.6%mth in August, above Westpac’s and the
market’s forecast for a 0.5%mth gain, leaving credit growth

running at 8.4%yr. Growth in housing credit continued to ease,
as we expected, though the mix highlights that the waning in
momentum is uneven, centred on investors rather than owner-
occupiers. Business credit meanwhile remains a source of
strength, notwithstanding some slowing recently.
For September, we expect credit growth to nudge to 0.5%mth,
with the divergence between housing credit and business
credit persisting.
AUS: Q3/Sep CPI (%qtr/%mth)

Oct 28, Quarter, Last: 0.6%, Westpac f/c: 1.3%,
Market f/c: 1.3%, Range: 0.6 to 1.4.
Oct 28, Month, Last: 0.4%, Westpac f/c: 0.6%,
Market f/c: 0.6 Range: 0.3 to 0.8
As noted in the review of the August CPI, our September
quarter CPI estimate is unchanged at 1.3%qtr holding the
annual pace flat at 3.9%yr. At the margin there was a 0.04ppt
increase due to a lift in auto fuel but it still rounds to 1.3%.
The seasonally adjusted CPI gains 1.1% highlighting that part

of the increase is due to usual seasonality.
In the month, the September CPI is expected to rise 0.6% with
the annual pace lifting to 4.1%yr. Recreation & culture make
the largest contribution but transport is not far behind. Our
seasonally adjusted estimate increases 0.5%.
For more details please see “ September CPI Preview “.
AUS: Q3/Sep Trimmed Mean (%qtr/mth)
Oct 28, Quarter, Last: 0.8%, Westpac f/c: 0.9%,
Market f/c: 1.0%, Range: 0.9 to 1.0.

Oct 28, Month, Last: 0.2%, Westpac f/c: 0.3%,
Market f/c: 0.3%, Range: 0.2 to 0.4
Our September quarter Trimmed Mean estimate is unchanged
at 0.9%qtr with the annual pace dipping a touch from 3.6%yr
to 3.5%yr. There was a small downward revision of 0.03ppt to
0.88% compared to our original published estimate but it still
rounds to 0.9%qtr.
In the month of September we are looking for a 0.3% in
the Trimmed Mean holding the annual pace steady at 3.6%

which will be the fourth consecutive month at this pace. The
distribution of the September Trimmed Mean ranges from
–0.3% to 0.7%.
For more details please see “ September CPI Preview “.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
NZ: RBNZ Official Cash Rate Review (%)
Oct 28, Last: 2.75, Westpac f/c: 3.00, Mkt f/c: 3.00
We expect the RBNZ to raise the OCR by 25bps to 3% at
the 28 October Review. Data since September show a higher
outlook for year-end inflation and a stronger GDP starting

point, but increased risks to domestic consumption and
residential investment. On balance, the medium-term inflation
picture will probably be judged to have worsened slightly
given recent movements in energy prices and the exchange
rate. A vote seems very possible as differences in view will
likely remain among the MPC about how much the medium-
term inflation outlook has really shifted in recent weeks.
Forward guidance will likely leave open the option to raise

the OCR further at the December Monetary Policy Statement
without presenting it as a sure thing.
NZ: Oct ANZ Business Confidence (index)
Oct 29, Last: 51.9
Firms remained optimistic in the September survey of business
confidence, with both the activity and inflation measures
broadly holding steady. However, it’s likely that most responses
were submitted in the early part of the month, while fuel prices
took a sharp step higher from the second half of September. The

survey comes too late for the RBNZ’s October policy review, but
evidence about the breadth of cost and price pressures could
have a bearing on the RBNZ’s thinking about the total amount of
policy tightening needed in the months ahead.
NZ: Sep Monthly Employment Indicator (%mth)
Oct 28, Last: 0.2, Westpac f/c: 0.3
The MEI has been gradually ticking higher in recent months,
showing that the jobs market has held its ground through
the Iran conflict and the consequent surge in fuel prices. The

weekly snapshots provided by Stats NZ have been running
ahead of year-ago levels and suggest another solid result for
September – we’ve pencilled in a 0.3% increase. Given the
history of this measure, however, both this month and the
previous month are likely to be revised down a little from their
initial release.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Oct FOMC meeting
Oct 27-28, Last: 3.875%, Mkt f/c: 3.875%, WBC f/c: 3.875%
Core CPI inflation and producer price inflation both surprised
to the upside in September, and the price of Brent oil has
risen back to around USD110. Underlying momentum in
the economy also remains robust, although employment

disappointed in the latest update. Given the concerns shown
by Committee members over price risks and their collective
resolve to return annual inflation to target, the above data
could easily justify a follow-up hike at the October meeting.
That said, Committee members who have spoken recently
have shown patience with respect to assessing the economy
and positioing policy. The market has responded, effectively
pricing out the chance of an October hike in favour of a

December move, which is also our expectation.
US Q3 GDP
Oct 29, %ann’d, Last: 2.2%, Mkt f/c: 3.0%, WBC f/c: 3.1%
The US economy continues to grow at a pace well above trend.
This outperformance is almost entirely due to the strength of
investment in data centres and other AI related assets. The
pipeline for this activity remains strong, limiting near-term
risks to the downside.
Abstracting from AI investment’s contribution, other business
investment and residential investment is weak, weighed down

by high term interest rates and uncertainty related to trade
policy. Household consumption growth is volatile month-to-
month, but the trend is solid. Absent a marked deterioration
in the labour market, which is not our expectation, this
momentum is likely to persist, as wealth continues to grow and
household income gains neutralises inflation. Come 2027 and
2028, growth is expected to slow to trend, but not below.

-
digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in bankruptcies and stable non
-
